We identify a pervasive failure mode we term \emph{Persona Collapse}: agents each assigned a distinct profile nonetheless converge into a narrow behavioral mode, producing a homogeneous simulated population. To quantify persona collapse, we propose a framework that measures how much of the persona space a population occupies (Coverage), how evenly agents spread across it (Uniformity), and how rich the resulting behavioral patterns are (Complexity). Evaluating ten LLMs on personality simulation (BFI-44), moral reasoning, and self-introduction, we observe persona collapse along two axes: (1) Dimensions: a model can appear diverse on one axis yet structurally degenerate on another, and (2) Domains: the same model may collapse the most in personality yet be the most diverse in moral reasoning. Furthermore, item-level diagnostics reveal that behavioral variation tracks coarse demographic stereotypes rather than the fine-grained individual differences specified in each persona. Counter-intuitively, \textbf{the models achieving the highest per-persona fidelity consistently produce the most stereotyped populations}. We release our toolkit and data to support population-level evaluation of LLMs.

</details>


### [254] [Green Shielding: A User-Centric Approach Towards Trustworthy AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24700)
*Aaron J. Li,Nicolas Sanchez,Hao Huang,Ruijiang Dong,Jaskaran Bains,Katrin Jaradeh,Zhen Xiang,Bo Li,Feng Liu,Aaron Kornblith,Bin Yu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出"绿色屏蔽"（Green Shielding）议程，通过构建基于真实用户查询的基准测试和扰动机制，系统研究良性输入变化如何影响LLM输出行为，并在医疗诊断场景中验证了提示层面的因素会导致模型输出在临床相关维度上产生类似帕累托最优的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有红队测试主要关注对抗性攻击，但忽略了日常、非对抗性的用户提问方式变化对LLM输出的显著影响，这在高风险部署场景中构成安全隐患。

Method: 提出CUE标准（真实上下文、真实效用指标、真实扰动），基于PCS框架与执业医师合作构建HealthCareMagic-Diagnosis基准，包含患者撰写的查询、结构化参考诊断集和临床导向指标，并设计多种扰动机制模拟日常输入变化。

Result: 在多个前沿LLM上的实验表明，提示层面的因素会沿临床相关维度改变模型输出行为，呈现帕累托式权衡；特别是"中性化"处理虽提高合理性和简洁性，但降低了对高风险和高概率疾病的覆盖。

Conclusion: 交互选择可系统性地改变模型输出的任务相关属性，相关工作可为高风险领域的安全部署提供用户导向的指导，且该议程可推广至其他决策支持和智能体系统。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed, yet their outputs can be highly sensitive to routine, non-adversarial variation in how users phrase queries, a gap not well addressed by existing red-teaming efforts. We propose Green Shielding, a user-centric agenda for building evidence-backed deployment guidance by characterizing how benign input variation shifts model behavior. We operationalize this agenda through the CUE criteria: benchmarks with authentic Context, reference standards and metrics that capture true Utility, and perturbations that reflect realistic variations in the Elicitation of model behavior. Guided by the PCS framework and developed with practicing physicians, we instantiate Green Shielding in medical diagnosis through HealthCareMagic-Diagnosis (HCM-Dx), a benchmark of patient-authored queries, together with structured reference diagnosis sets and clinically grounded metrics for evaluating differential diagnosis lists. We also study perturbation regimes that capture routine input variation and show that prompt-level factors shift model behavior along clinically meaningful dimensions. Across multiple frontier LLMs, these shifts trace out Pareto-like tradeoffs. In particular, neutralization, which removes common user-level factors while preserving clinical content, increases plausibility and yields more concise, clinician-like differentials, but reduces coverage of highly likely and safety-critical conditions. Together, these results show that interaction choices can systematically shift task-relevant properties of model outputs and support user-facing guidance for safer deployment in high-stakes domains. Although instantiated here in medical diagnosis, the agenda extends naturally to other decision-support settings and agentic AI systems.

</details>


<div id='cs.GR'></div>

# cs.GR [[Back]](#toc)

### [255] [Distance Field Rasterization for End-to-End Mesh Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.23537)
*Jinkai Cui,Kaiwen Song,Chumeng Niu,Juyong Zhang*

Main category: cs.GR

TL;DR: 提出SDFRaster，一种可光栅化的有符号距离场表示，结合光栅化效率和SDF的明确表面定义，通过四面体格网优化连续SDF并集成可微行进四面体实现端到端网格重建，无需后处理提取。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 光栅化方法虽有实时新视角合成能力，但缺乏全局一致的表面表示，表面提取依赖启发式后处理；隐式SDF方法虽提供明确表面，但通常需要昂贵的射线步进优化。现有方法无法同时兼顾效率和表面质量。

Method: 基于Delaunay四面体化构建四面体格网，优化其上的连续SDF；通过光栅化四面体并Alpha混合其贡献实现高效渲染；将可微行进四面体（Differentiable Marching Tetrahedra）集成到优化循环中，实现端到端网格重建。

Result: 在DTU和Tanks and Temples数据集上，SDFRaster相比现有方法实现了更高质量、更完整的表面重建，且存储成本更低。

Conclusion: SDFRaster成功弥合了光栅化效率与隐式SDF表面质量之间的差距，提供了一种既能高效渲染又能端到端重建网格的统一表示方法。

Abstract: Rasterization based methods have recently enabled high-quality novel view synthesis at real-time rates, but their underlying volumetric primitives do not expose a direct, globally consistent surface representation, leaving sur face extraction to heuristic post-processing. In contrast, implicit signed dis tance field (SDF) methods provide well-defined surfaces but are typically optimized with computationally expensive ray marching. We propose SD FRaster, a rasterizable SDF representation that bridges this gap by combin ing the efficiency of rasterization with signed distance field for end-to-end mesh reconstruction. Starting from a Delaunay tetrahedralization, we op timize a continuous SDF over a tetrahedral grid and render it efficiently by rasterizing tetrahedra and alpha-compositing their contributions. We further integrate differentiable Marching Tetrahedra into the optimization loop, enablingend-to-endmeshreconstructionwithoutpost-processingmesh extraction. Experiments on DTU and Tanks and Temples demonstrate that SDFRaster achieves higher-quality and more complete surface reconstruc tions with lower storage cost than state-of-the-art approaches. Project page: https://ustc3dv.github.io/SDFRaster/

</details>


### [256] [Progressive-Iterative Fairing of Curves and Surfaces with Localized Control Point Adjustment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.23590)
*Jia Lin,Hongwei Lin,Weixian Huang,Azan Zhang*

Main category: cs.GR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于控制点调整的渐进迭代光顺方法，通过为每个控制点分配独立权重，实现全局和局部形状的精细光顺，并提供了自动控制点选择方法，消除了手动交互需求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统曲面光顺方法多采用全局修改，缺乏细粒度控制能力，无法满足产品质量、物理性能和美学方面对局部精确调整的需求。

Method: 提出渐进迭代光顺方法，为每个控制点分配独立权重以支持精确局部形状调整；同时设计自动控制点选择方法以消除手动交互依赖。

Result: 数值实验证明了该方法在效率和有效性方面的优越性能。

Conclusion: 该方法兼具全局与局部光顺能力，实现了对光顺效果的精细控制，并摆脱了对手动交互的依赖。

Abstract: Curve and surface fairing is crucial in computer-aided geometric design, influencing product quality, physical performance, and aesthetics. Traditional methods often apply global modifications, lacking fine-grained control. This paper introduces a novel progressive-iterative fairing method based on control point adjustment. By assigning independent weights to each control point, our approach enables precise, localized shape adjustments. The method functions both globally and locally, allowing for comprehensive shape fairing and fine control over the fairing effect. Furthermore, this paper provides an automatic control point selection method to adjust shapes, thereby eliminating the reliance on manual interaction. Numerical experiments demonstrate the efficiency and effectiveness of our approach.

</details>


### [257] [From Visual Synthesis to Interactive Worlds: Toward Production-Ready 3D Asset Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.23629)
*Jiafeng Wu,Zhuofan Lou,Jian Liu,Dazhao Du,Chunchao Guo,Song Guo*

Main category: cs.GR

TL;DR: 本综述围绕3D内容生成的资产生产管线，提出“三维资产层级（通用物体/角色/场景）× 生产生命周期（数据基础/几何合成/拓扑优化/UV展开/PBR外观/骨骼绑定/场景组装）”的二维分类法，系统评估了当前方法在引擎级约束下的可用性差距，并总结了评估指标与开放挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 游戏开发、具身AI、世界模拟、数字孪生和空间计算等领域对可部署于实时交互环境的3D内容需求激增，但现有生成方法的输出与生产级标准之间存在持续差距。现有综述多按算法族系组织，未能有效评估输出在引擎中的直接可用性，因此需要从资产生产管线视角进行系统性梳理。

Method: 提出二维分类法：水平轴区分三个资产层级（通用物体、角色、场景），垂直轴沿生产生命周期追踪每个层级的关键环节（数据基础、几何合成、拓扑优化、UV展开、PBR外观、骨骼绑定、场景组装），据此评估方法的生成能力与直接可用性，并整合几何保真度、外观质量、资产可用性、场景级物理合理性等评估指标与协议。

Result: 系统呈现了当前方法在各资产层级和生产环节上的进展与局限，揭示了从“生成视觉上合理的形状”到“产出满足引擎级约束的可用资产”之间的具体差距，并建立了涵盖几何、外观、可用性、物理合理性等多维度的评估体系。

Conclusion: 指出数据质量、生成可控性、端到端资产化、物理接地生成等开放挑战，并将生产就绪型3D内容定位为新兴交互世界模型和具身智能系统的基础设施。

Abstract: Three-dimensional content generation has progressed from producing isolated, visually plausible shapes to constructing structured assets that can be deployed in real-time interactive environments. This trajectory is driven by converging demands from game development, embodied AI, world simulation, digital twins, and spatial computing, all of which require 3D content that goes beyond surface appearance to satisfy engine-level constraints on topology, UV parameterization, physically based materials, skeletal rigging, and physics-aware scene layout. Despite rapid advances in generative modeling, a persistent gap separates the outputs of current methods from the production-ready standard expected by interactive applications. This survey addresses that gap by organizing the literature around the asset production pipeline rather than algorithmic families. Along the horizontal axis we distinguish three asset tiers, namely general objects, characters, and scenes, while the vertical axis traces each tier through the full production lifecycle from data foundations and geometry synthesis through topology optimization, UV unwrapping, PBR appearance, rigging, and scene assembly. Through this two-dimensional taxonomy we assess not only what current methods can generate but whether their outputs are directly usable in downstream engines and simulation platforms. We further consolidate evaluation metrics and protocols that span geometric fidelity, appearance quality, asset usability, and scene-level physical plausibility. The survey concludes by identifying open challenges in data quality, generation controllability, end-to-end assetization, and physically grounded generation, and by situating production-ready 3D content as foundational infrastructure for emerging interactive world models and embodied intelligent systems.

</details>


### [258] [Personalizing Causal Audio-Driven Facial Motion via Dynamic Multi-modal Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.23692)
*Xuangeng Chu,Yu Han,Wei Mao,Shih-En Wei*

Main category: cs.GR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种端到端的因果框架，通过动态多模态风格检索实现音频驱动的面部动画个性化生成，在保持超低延迟的同时显著提升了唇形同步精度、身份一致性和感知真实感。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有音频驱动面部动画框架无法兼顾实时流传输与高保真个性化：要么依赖引入延迟的音频前瞻技术，要么需要用户高度配合预编码静态嵌入，无法捕捉动态特征。

Method: 提出两项核心创新：(1) 时间层级运动表示——在保持解码因果性的同时捕获全局时间上下文与高频细节；(2) 多模态风格检索器——联合查询音频和运动动态提取风格先验，不破坏因果性，支持可扩展个性化且对模板数量和内容完全灵活。

Result: 集成在因果自回归架构中，在唇形同步精度、身份一致性和感知真实感上显著优于现有方法，经定量评估和用户研究验证。

Conclusion: 该因果框架成功解决了实时性与个性化之间的权衡，通过动态多模态风格检索实现了低延迟、高保真的面部动画生成。

Abstract: Audio-driven facial animation is essential for immersive digital interaction, yet existing frameworks fail to reconcile real-time streaming with high-fidelity personalization. Current methods often rely on latency-inducing audio look-ahead, or require high user compliance to pre-encode static embeddings that fails to capture dynamic idiosyncrasies. We present an end-to-end causal framework for personalizing causal facial motion generation via dynamic multi-modal style retrieval, enabling ultra-low latency while uniquely leveraging unstructured style references. We introduce two key innovations: (1) a temporal hierarchical motion representation that captures global temporal context and high-frequency details while maintaining decoding causality, and (2) a multi-modal style retriever that jointly queries audio and motion to dynamically extract stylistic priors without breaking causality. This mechanism allows for scalable personalization with total flexibility regarding the number and contents of templates. By integrating these components into a causal autoregressive architecture, our method significantly outperforms state-of-the-art approaches in lip-sync accuracy, identity consistency, and perceived realism, supported by extensive quantitative evaluations and user studies.

</details>


### [259] [Prox-E: Fine-Grained 3D Shape Editing via Primitive-Based Abstractions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.23774)
*Etai Sella,Hao Phung,Nitay Amiel,Or Litany,Or Patashnik,Hadar Averbuch-Elor*

Main category: cs.GR

TL;DR: Prox-E是一种无需训练的框架，通过基元几何抽象实现细粒度3D编辑，在保持物体整体身份的同时进行局部结构修改，性能优于现有2D-based和training-based方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于2D图像编辑模型的3D编辑方法在处理细粒度3D编辑时存在局限，难以在应用局部结构变化的同时严格保持物体的整体身份。

Method: 首先将输入3D形状抽象为紧凑的几何基元集合；然后利用预训练的视觉语言模型(VLM)编辑该抽象结构，指定基元级别的变化；最后将这些结构编辑结果引导3D生成模型，实现细粒度局部修改，同时保留原始形状未修改区域。

Result: 实验表明Prox-E在身份保持、形状质量和指令保真度之间实现了更有效的平衡，优于多种现有方法，包括2D-based 3D编辑器和基于训练的方法。

Conclusion: 提出了一种训练自由的细粒度3D编辑框架，通过显式的基元几何抽象实现了对局部结构变化的精确控制，有效解决了现有2D-centric方法在3D编辑中的身份保持难题。

Abstract: Text-based 2D image editing models have recently reached an impressive level of maturity, motivating a growing body of work that heavily depends on these models to drive 3D edits. While effective for appearance-based modifications, such 2D-centric 3D editing pipelines often struggle with fine-grained 3D editing, where localized structural changes must be applied while strictly preserving an object's overall identity. To address this limitation, we propose Prox-E, a training-free framework that enables fine-grained 3D control through an explicit, primitive-based geometric abstraction. Our framework first abstracts an input 3D shape into a compact set of geometric primitives. A pretrained vision-language model (VLM) then edits this abstraction to specify primitive-level changes. These structural edits are subsequently used to guide a 3D generative model, enabling fine-grained, localized modifications while preserving unchanged regions of the original shape. Through extensive experiments, we demonstrate that our method consistently balances identity preservation, shape quality, and instruction fidelity more effectively than various existing approaches, including 2D-based 3D editors and training-based methods.

</details>


### [260] [MUSIC: Learning Muscle-Driven Dexterous Hand Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.23886)
*Pei Xu,Yufei Ye,Shuchun Sun,Yu Ding,Elizabeth Schumann,C. Karen Liu*

Main category: cs.GR

TL;DR: 提出了一种数据驱动的、基于物理的肌肉驱动灵巧控制方法，使肌肉骨骼手能够执行精确的钢琴演奏。该方法采用分层架构，结合高频肌肉级控制与低频潜在空间协调，通过强化学习训练通用单手策略，蒸馏为VAE模型，并训练特定曲目策略在潜在空间中协调双手运动，达到超越参考数据的演奏效果。实验表明该方法在多风格曲目中实现了最先进的物理控制性能，且肌肉骨骼手模型在生物力学稳定性和追踪精度上优于现有模型，能生成与人类EMG记录一致的生理激活模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有物理仿真中的灵巧手控制方法难以实现精细的、类似人类的钢琴演奏动作，尤其是肌肉驱动的多指协调与双手协同。研究者希望通过数据驱动的方式，结合强化学习与潜在空间学习，使肌肉骨骼手能够演奏超出参考数据集的新曲目，并生成与真实人体生理信号一致的肌肉激活模式。

Method: 采用分层架构：低层通过强化学习训练通用单手策略，在追踪大量参考运动轨迹的同时生成动态肌肉-肌腱激活；然后将追踪策略蒸馏为VAE模型，形成平滑结构化的潜在空间以抽象底层肌肉动力学；高层训练特定曲目策略在此潜在空间中操作，根据乐谱音符事件协调双手运动。同时提出了增强的肌肉骨骼手模型以支持精细手指控制。

Result: 该方法在多种音乐风格的钢琴曲目上实现协调的双手运动与准确的按键，达到物理灵巧控制中钢琴演奏的最先进水平。肌肉骨骼手模型在生物力学稳定性和追踪精度上优于现有模型，且能生成与人类EMG记录一致的生理学上合理的激活模式。

Conclusion: 数据驱动的分层控制架构与增强的肌肉骨骼手模型相结合，可以有效解决精细灵巧操作问题，实现超越参考数据的新曲目演奏，并生成生理上可信的肌肉激活模式。

Abstract: We present a data-driven approach for physics-based, muscle-driven dexterous control that enables musculoskeletal hands to perform precise piano playing for novel pieces of music outside the reference dataset. Our approach combines high-frequency muscle-level control with low-frequency latent-space coordination in a hierarchical architecture. At the low level, general single-hand policies are trained via reinforcement learning to generate dynamic muscle-tendon activations while tracking trajectories from a large reference motion dataset. The resulting tracking policies are then distilled into variational autoencoder (VAE) models, yielding smooth and structured latent spaces that abstract away low-level muscle dynamics. For the high level, we train piece-specific policies to operate in this latent space, coordinating bimanual motions based on specific goals, denoted by note events extracted from given musical scores, to synthesize performances beyond the reference data. In addition, we present an enhanced musculoskeletal hand model that supports fine control of fingers for accurate low-level motion tracking and diverse high-level motion synthesis. We evaluate the control pipeline of our approach on a diverse piano repertoire spanning multiple musical styles and technical demands. Results demonstrate that our approach can synthesize coordinated bimanual motions with accurate key presses, and achieve the state-of-the-art performance of piano playing in physics-based dexterous control. We also show that our musculoskeletal hand model demonstrates superior biomechanical stability and tracking precision compared to the existing model, and validate that our musculoskeletal hand model and muscle-driven controller can generate physiologically plausible activation patterns that align with human electromyography (EMG) recordings.

</details>


### [261] [Neural Enhancement of Analytical Appearance Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24081)
*Xuanzhe Shen,Xiaohe Ma,Kun Zhou,Hongzhi Wu*

Main category: cs.GR

TL;DR: 本文提出神经增强框架，通过用小型MLP替换解析反射模型中的关键计算节点，在保持紧凑性和可解释性的同时提升表达精度，经超立方体搜索自动优化替换位置，最终得到准确、紧凑、高效且兼容标准渲染管线的混合模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统解析反射模型紧凑可解释但精度不足，神经模型拟合能力强但泛化性差且存储评估成本高，两者各有限制，需结合二者优势。

Method: 提出神经增强框架，用小型多层感知器替换输入解析外观模型的关键计算节点/算子；采用基于超立方体的可微搜索自动高效识别待替换节点，以最大化增益。

Result: 增强后的常见解析BRDF模型同时具备准确性、紧凑性和高效性，在拟合实测反射率和双向纹理函数任务上优于现有最先进方法，且兼容标准光栅化/光线追踪管线。

Conclusion: 神经增强框架成功融合解析模型与神经模型的优势，以适度成本显著提升解析模型表达力，为反射建模提供了一种实用且高效的混合方案。

Abstract: Traditional analytical reflectance models, while compact and interpretable, lack the capacity to accurately represent physical measurements. Recent neural models, which closely fit input data, are less generalizable and often more expensive to store and evaluate. To combine the strengths and overcome the limitations of these two classes of models, we present neural enhancement, a novel framework to boost an input analytical appearance model, by identifying and replacing its key computational nodes/operators with small-scale multi-layer perceptrons. This allows us to leverage the computational graph structure of the original model, while improving its expressiveness at a modest cost. To make the enhancement computationally tractable, we propose a hypercube-based search to automatically and efficiently identify the node(s) and/or operator(s) to be replaced towards maximal gain in a differentiable fashion. We enhance a number of common analytical BRDF models. The results are, at once accurate, compact and efficient, and compare favorably with state-of-the-art work on fitting measured reflectance and bidirectional texture functions. Finally, our models are fully compatible with any standard rasterization or ray-tracing pipeline.

</details>


### [262] [Large-Scale Photogrammetric Documentation of St. John's Co-Cathedral: A Workflow for Cultural Heritage Preservation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24316)
*Matthew Kenely,Mark Bugeja,Andre Grima,Peter Pullicino,Matthew Pullicino,Dylan Seychell*

Main category: cs.GR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一套完整的、可复现的大规模摄影测量工作流程，用于对圣约翰联合大教堂（世界遗产）进行高精度三维数字化存档，整合了DSLR、无人机和LIDAR多模态数据，生成了250-300亿三角形的3D重建模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文化遗产面临自然灾害、人为破坏和时间侵蚀的风险，需要高精度的数字化备份用于灾后恢复、保护分析和学术研究。同时，巴洛克建筑中反光金属表面、深色材料、复杂挂毯和受限访问等特性给传统摄影测量带来了独特挑战，亟需一套系统性的解决方案。

Method: 在七个夜间采集时段内，使用DSLR相机、无人机摄影和LIDAR扫描采集99,000张图像；采用策略性图像分级和AI辅助去噪处理低光照颗粒噪声；进行大规模LIDAR点云清理；使用RealityCapture进行混合摄影测量重建；采用网格细分策略优化实时可视化；并初步实验了高斯泼溅（Gaussian Splatting）作为补充表示技术。

Result: 成功生成了包含250-300亿三角形的高细节3D重建模型，建立了服务于灾后恢复文档、保护分析、虚拟旅游和学术研究的数字档案。

Conclusion: 该工作流有效解决了巴洛克建筑遗产数字化中的实际挑战（尤其是反光表面问题），为文化遗产专业人员提供了可复制的技术路线，并指出了高斯泼溅作为未来研究方向。

Abstract: We present a comprehensive methodology for the large-scale photogrammetric documentation of St. John's Co-Cathedral in Valletta, Malta, a UNESCO World Heritage site renowned for its ornate Baroque architecture and Caravaggio masterpieces. Over seven nights of evening-only data collection, we captured 99,000 images using DSLR cameras, drone photography, and LIDAR scanning to create a highly detailed 3D reconstruction comprising 25-30 billion triangles. This paper documents our complete workflow for cultural heritage preservation, addressing the unique challenges of digitizing complex baroque architectural spaces with highly reflective metallic surfaces, dark materials, intricate tapestries, and restricted access. We detail our pipeline from multi-modal data acquisition through processing, including strategic image grading and AI-assisted denoising to address low-light grain, extensive LIDAR point cloud cleanup, hybrid photogrammetric reconstruction using RealityCapture, and mesh subdivision strategies for real-time visualization engines. Our methodology combines automated workflows with necessary manual intervention to handle the scale and complexity of the project, with particular attention to reflective surface challenges characteristic of baroque heritage sites. We also present preliminary experiments with Gaussian splatting as a complementary representation technique. The resulting digital archive serves multiple preservation purposes including disaster recovery documentation, conservation analysis, virtual tourism, and scholarly research. This work provides a detailed, replicable workflow for heritage professionals undertaking similar large-scale architectural documentation projects, addressing the practical challenges of applying photogrammetric methods in complex real-world heritage scenarios.

</details>


### [263] [Voxel Deformation-Aware Neural Intersection Function](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24666)
*Chih-Chen Kao,Grzegorz Makowski,Shin Fujieda,Takahiro Harada*

Main category: cs.GR

TL;DR: 本文扩展了局部细分神经相交函数（LSNIF），通过引入基于无网格渲染的静止空间与变形空间公式，支持参数化可变形和动画几何。该方法在无需重新训练的情况下，利用尺度不变距离回归、不确定性加权多任务学习和混合位置网格编码，实现了对动态几何的鲁棒神经相交预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将LSNIF从静态几何扩展至参数化可变形和动画几何，解决动态几何在神经隐式表示中的相交预测问题。

Method: 引入rest-space和deformed-space双空间公式；采用尺度不变距离回归、不确定性加权多任务学习、混合位置网格编码；基于无网格渲染的映射机制将射线样本映射回规范空间。

Result: 保持LSNIF的紧凑性和效率，同时实现对动态几何的鲁棒神经相交预测。

Conclusion: 通过双空间公式和变形感知训练策略，有效实现了无需重新训练的动态几何神经相交函数。

Abstract: We extend the Locally-Subdivided Neural Intersection Function (LSNIF) to support parameterized deformable and animated geometry. Our approach introduces a rest-space and deformed-space formulation inspired by meshless rendering, allowing ray samples to be mapped back to a canonical space where a single neural network represents geometry consistently across poses without retraining. To maintain accuracy under deformation-aware training, we incorporate scale-invariant distance regression, uncertainty-weighted multi-task learning, and a hybrid positional-grid encoding. The resulting method preserves the compactness and efficiency of LSNIF while enabling robust neural intersection prediction for dynamic geometry.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [264] [PExA: Parallel Exploration Agent for Complex Text-to-SQL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22934)
*Tanmay Parekh,Ella Hofmann-Coyle,Shuyi Wang,Sachith Sri Ram Kothur,Srivas Prasad,Yunmo Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于软件测试覆盖的文本到SQL生成框架，将原始查询分解为并行执行的简单原子SQL测试用例，在确保语义覆盖后生成最终SQL，在Spider 2.0基准上达到70.2%的执行准确率，创下新SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM的text-to-SQL智能体常在延迟与性能之间面临权衡困境，性能提升往往以增加延迟为代价，反之亦然。作者希望打破这种trade-off，在不牺牲延迟的前提下提升SQL生成质量。

Method: 将text-to-SQL生成重新建模为软件测试覆盖问题：为原始查询准备一组测试用例，包含若干更简单、原子化的SQL子查询，这些子查询可并行执行；通过迭代覆盖测试用例，累积足够信息后，利用已探索的测试SQL来生成最终SQL语句。

Result: 在Spider 2.0基准测试上取得了70.2%的执行准确率，达到新的最先进水平。

Conclusion: 通过测试覆盖驱动的并行化原子SQL探索与信息积累，可以有效改善text-to-SQL生成的延迟-性能平衡，验证了该框架的有效性。

Abstract: LLM-based agents for text-to-SQL often struggle with latency-performance trade-off, where performance improvements come at the cost of latency or vice versa. We reformulate text-to-SQL generation within the lens of software test coverage where the original query is prepared with a suite of test cases with simpler, atomic SQLs that are executed in parallel and together ensure semantic coverage of the original query. After iterating on test case coverage, the final SQL is generated only when enough information is gathered, leveraging the explored test case SQLs to ground the final generation. We validated our framework on a state-of-the-art benchmark for text-to-SQL, Spider 2.0, achieving a new state-of-the-art with 70.2% execution accuracy.

</details>


### [265] [The Power of Power Law: Asymmetry Enables Compositional Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22951)
*Zixuan Wang,Xingyu Dang,Jason D. Lee,Kaifeng Lyu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文发现，在组合推理任务中，幂律分布的数据训练效果优于均匀分布，原因是幂律采样能改善损失景观，使模型先学习高频技能组合，再高效掌握低频长尾技能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探讨在自然语言数据呈幂律分布的背景下，是否应该通过重加权或筛选数据使其趋于均匀分布来帮助模型更好地学习低频长尾技能。

Method: 通过广泛组合推理任务（状态追踪、多步算术）进行实验对比，并设计了一个极简的技能组合任务进行理论分析，证明幂律分布在训练数据量上具有显著优势。

Result: 幂律分布训练在组合推理任务上一致优于均匀分布训练；理论分析表明幂律采样能诱导有益的非对称性，改善病理性的损失景观。

Conclusion: 提出了一种关于有效数据分布的反直觉观点：保留自然数据的幂律分布特性反而更有利于模型学习，尤其是对长尾技能的学习。

Abstract: Natural language data follows a power-law distribution, with most knowledge and skills appearing at very low frequency. While a common intuition suggests that reweighting or curating data towards a uniform distribution may help models better learn these long-tail skills, we find a counterintuitive result: across a wide range of compositional reasoning tasks, such as state tracking and multi-step arithmetic, training under power-law distributions consistently outperforms training under uniform distributions. To understand this advantage, we introduce a minimalist skill-composition task and show that learning under a power-law distribution provably requires significantly less training data. Our theoretical analysis reveals that power law sampling induces a beneficial asymmetry that improves the pathological loss landscape, which enables models to first acquire high-frequency skill compositions with low data complexity, which in turn serves as a stepping stone to efficiently learn rare long-tailed skills. Our results offer an alternative perspective on what constitutes an effective data distribution for training models.

</details>


### [266] [On the Existence of an Inverse Solution for Preference-Based Reductions in Argumentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22958)
*Alessio Zaninotto,Bruno Yun,Nir Oren,Srdjan Vesic*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了偏好论证框架（PAFs）的逆问题：给定论证图、标记和语义，判断是否存在能产生该标记的偏好关系。在完全语义下，针对四种偏好约简方法，证明了该问题在大多数情况下可在多项式时间内求解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 偏好论证框架（PAFs）通过偏好关系将攻击转化为击败，但现有研究主要关注正向推理（给定偏好求标记）。逆向问题（给定标记求偏好关系）在偏好 elicitation 和可解释性等领域有重要应用，此前尚未被系统研究。

Method: 以完全语义（complete semantics）为背景，针对四种最常用的偏好约简方法（将PAF转化为AAF的方式），将逆向问题形式化为决策问题：给定论证图、标记和语义，判断是否存在偏好关系使得该标记在对应AAF中成立。通过构造性算法和复杂度分析来判定。

Result: 对于四种偏好约简方法中的大部分情形，该逆问题可以在多项式时间内求解。具体而言，通过设计多项式时间的判定算法，证明了问题在大多数情况下的计算复杂度是低的。

Conclusion: 偏好论证框架的逆问题在完全语义下对多数约简方法是多项式时间可解的，这为偏好学习、可解释推理等应用提供了理论基础。

Abstract: Preference-based argumentation frameworks (PAFs) extend Dung's approach to abstract argumentation (AAFs) by encoding preferences over arguments. Such preferences control the transformation of attacks into defeats, and different approaches to doing so result in different reductions from a PAF to an AAF. In this paper we consider a PAF inverse problem which takes an argumentation graph, a labelling and a semantics as an input, and outputs a ``yes" or ``no" as to whether there is a preference relation between the arguments which can yield the desired labelling. This inverse problem has applications in areas including preference elicitation and explainability. We consider this problem in the context of the four most widely-used preference based reductions under the complete semantics. We show that in most cases, the problem can be answered in polynomial time.

</details>


### [267] [FormalScience: Scalable Human-in-the-Loop Autoformalisation of Science with Agentic Code Generation in Lean](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.23002)
*Jordan Meadows,Lan Zhang,Andre Freitas*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出FormalScience——一个领域无关的人机协作智能体管线，使领域专家（无需深厚形式化语言经验）能以低成本将非正式的科学推理转化为语法正确、语义对齐的形式化证明。基于该管线构建了包含200道大学物理问题的FormalPhysics数据集（Lean4形式化表示），并系统刻画了物理自动形式化中的语义漂移现象（如记法坍缩、抽象提升）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将非正式的数学推理自动形式化为可验证代码对LLM是重大挑战，而在物理学等科学领域中，狄拉克符号、向量微积分等特定领域形式化方法带来了额外困难，现有LLM和智能体方法尚未解决这些问题。

Method: 提出FormalScience领域无关人机协作智能体管线，让单个领域专家无需深厚形式化语言经验即可产出形式化证明。基于该管线构建FormalPhysics数据集（200道量子力学和电磁学问题），并评估了零样本提示、自我修正反馈和多阶段智能体方法等自动形式化方法。

Result: FormalPhysics数据集实现了完美的形式化有效性，且语句复杂度高于现有形式化数学基准。首次系统刻画了物理自动形式化中的语义漂移，包括记法坍缩和抽象提升等概念。

Conclusion: 揭示了当完全语义保留不可达时形式化语言所验证的内容，并开源了代码库及交互式UI系统，可推广至物理之外的科学领域自动形式化和定理证明。

Abstract: Formalising informal mathematical reasoning into formally verifiable code is a significant challenge for large language models. In scientific fields such as physics, domain-specific machinery (\textit{e.g.} Dirac notation, vector calculus) imposes additional formalisation challenges that modern LLMs and agentic approaches have yet to tackle. To aid autoformalisation in scientific domains, we present FormalScience; a domain-agnostic human-in-the-loop agentic pipeline that enables a single domain expert (without deep formal language experience) to produce \textit{syntactically correct} and \textit{semantically aligned} formal proofs of informal reasoning for low economic cost. Applying FormalScience to physics, we construct FormalPhysics, a dataset of 200 university-level (LaTeX) physics problems and solutions (primarily quantum mechanics and electromagnetism), along with their Lean4 formal representations. Compared to existing formal math benchmarks, FormalPhysics achieves perfect formal validity and exhibits greater statement complexity. We evaluate open-source models and proprietary systems on a statement autoformalisation task on our dataset via zero-shot prompting, self-refinement with error feedback, and a novel multi-stage agentic approach, and explore autoformalisation limitations in modern LLM-based approaches. We provide the first systematic characterisation of semantic drift in physics autoformalisation in terms of concepts such as notational collapse and abstraction elevation which reveals what formal language verifies when full semantic preservation is unattainable. We release the codebase together with an interactive UI-based FormalScience system which facilitates autoformalisation and theorem proving in scientific domains beyond physics.https://github.com/jmeadows17/formal-science

</details>


### [268] [A Systematic Approach for Large Language Models Debugging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.23027)
*Basel Shbita,Anna Lisa Gentile,Bing Zhang,Sungeun An,Shailja Thakur,Shubhi Asthana,Yi Zhou,Saptha Surendran,Farhan Ahmed,Rohan Kulkarni,Yuya Jeremy Ong,Chad DeLuca,Hima Patel*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一套系统性的大语言模型调试方法，将模型视为可观测系统，通过结构化的、模型无关的流程（从问题检测到模型优化）来诊断和修复LLM的各类错误。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM因其黑箱和概率特性，调试极其困难；现有方法碎片化，缺乏统一的、可复现的系统性调试框架。

Method: 将评估、可解释性和错误分析实践统一起来，形成一个迭代的、模型无关的调试流程，涵盖问题检测、提示词与参数优化、微调数据适配等环节。

Result: 该方法在缺乏标准化基准和评估标准的场景下依然有效，能够加速故障排查，提升系统的可复现性、透明度和可扩展性。

Conclusion: 结构化的调试方法论对于LLM系统的可靠部署至关重要，它能够弥补当前调试实践的不足，推动LLM应用的工程化落地。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have become central to modern AI workflows, powering applications from open-ended text generation to complex agent-based reasoning. However, debugging these models remains a persistent challenge due to their opaque and probabilistic nature and the difficulty of diagnosing errors across diverse tasks and settings. This paper introduces a systematic approach for LLM debugging that treats models as observable systems, providing structured, model-agnostic methods from issue detection to model refinement. By unifying evaluation, interpretability, and error-analysis practices, our approach enables practitioners to iteratively diagnose model weaknesses, refine prompts and model parameters, and adapt data for fine-tuning or assessment, while remaining effective in contexts where standardized benchmarks and evaluation criteria are lacking. We argue that such a structured methodology not only accelerates troubleshooting but also fosters reproducibility, transparency, and scalability in the deployment of LLM-based systems.

</details>


### [269] [A Decoupled Human-in-the-Loop System for Controlled Autonomy in Agentic Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.23049)
*Edward Cheng,Jeshua Cheng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种解耦的 Human-in-the-Loop (HITL) 系统架构，将人类监督作为独立组件嵌入智能体运行环境，并引入四维设计框架（干预条件、角色解析、交互语义、通信通道）以支持可扩展、一致且协议级的人类监督集成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 HITL 机制通常嵌入在应用逻辑中，限制了跨多智能体环境的复用性、一致性和可扩展性；需要一种解耦且结构化的方法来实现可扩展的治理与渐进式自主性。

Method: 1) 提出解耦的 HITL 系统架构，将人类监督作为独立系统组件；2) 通过显式接口和结构化执行模型分离人类交互管理与应用工作流；3) 引入四维设计框架：干预条件、角色解析、交互语义、通信通道；4) 支持与智能体通信协议对齐，实现协议级 HITL 实现。

Result: 该设计能够实现选择性且上下文感知的人类参与，同时保持系统级一致性；支持协议级 HITL 实现，为智能体工作流中的可扩展治理和渐进式自主性提供基础。

Conclusion: 通过外部化 HITL 并结构化其集成，该方法为多智能体环境中的人类监督提供了可复用、一致且可扩展的架构基础，有助于实现渐进式自主的安全控制。

Abstract: AI agents are increasingly deployed to execute tasks and make decisions within agentic workflows, introducing new requirements for safe and controlled autonomy. Prior work has established the importance of human oversight for ensuring transparency, accountability, and trustworthiness in such systems. However, existing implementations of Human-in-the-Loop (HITL) mechanisms are typically embedded within application logic, limiting reuse, consistency, and scalability across multi-agent environments.
  This paper presents a decoupled HITL system architecture that treats human oversight as an independent system component within the agent operating environment. The proposed design separates human interaction management from application workflows through explicit interfaces and a structured execution model. In addition, a design framework is introduced to formalize HITL integration along four dimensions: intervention conditions, role resolution, interaction semantics, and communication channel. This framework enables selective and context-aware human involvement while maintaining system-level consistency.
  The approach supports alignment with emerging agent communication protocols, allowing HITL to be implemented as a protocol-level concern. By externalizing HITL and structuring its integration, the system provides a foundation for scalable governance and progressive autonomy in agentic workflows.

</details>


### [270] [Don't Make the LLM Read the Graph: Make the Graph Think](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.23057)
*Yuqi Sun,Tianqin Meng,George Liu,Yashraj Panwar,Lakshya Chaudhry,Munasib Ilham,Aman Chadha*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 通过3000+次对照实验，本文研究了显式信念图在LLM多智能体协作推理中的作用，发现：集成架构决定信念图价值（作为提示上下文仅对弱模型有益，作为动作排序门控则对强模型也至关重要）；识别出"规划者抗命"这一模型家族特异性失败模式（Llama 70B 90%覆盖推荐，Gemini几乎为零）；完整游戏证据表明智能体间约定优于所有单智能体干预；深度信念图存在收益递减现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究显式信念图是否能提升LLM在多智能体协作推理中的表现，以及影响其有效性的关键因素。

Method: 在合作纸牌游戏Hanabi中，针对四个LLM家族进行3000+次受控试验，测试不同的信念图集成架构（提示上下文 vs. 动作排序门控）、不同ToM阶数、不同智能体数量下的表现。

Result: 1) 信念图作为提示上下文时仅对弱模型有益（2阶ToM 80% vs 10%，OR=36.0），作为动作排序门控时对所有模型都至关重要（100% vs 20%）；2) 发现"规划者抗命"现象：Llama 70B 90%覆盖正确推荐，Gemini几乎为零；3) 智能体间约定提升128%（p=0.003），优于所有单智能体干预；4) 浅层信念图性价比最优，深度信念图在5玩家时出现负面效果（-1.5分，p=0.029）。

Conclusion: 信念图对LLM多智能体协作的价值取决于集成架构而非模型能力；浅层信念图更实用，深度信念图可能有害；智能体间约定是提升协作性能的关键。

Abstract: We investigate whether explicit belief graphs improve LLM performance in cooperative multi-agent reasoning. Through 3,000+ controlled trials across four LLM families in the cooperative card game Hanabi, we establish four findings. First, integration architecture determines whether belief graphs provide value: as prompt context, graphs are decorative for strong models and beneficial only for weak models on 2nd-order Theory of Mind (80% vs 10%, p<0.0001, OR=36.0); when graphs gate action selection through ranked shortlists, they become structurally essential even for strong models (100% vs 20% on 2nd-order ToM, p<0.001). Second, we identify "Planner Defiance," a model-family-specific failure where LLMs override correct planner recommendations at partial competence (90% override, replicated N=20); Gemini models show near-zero defiance while Llama 70B shows 90%, and models distinguish factual context (deferred to) from advisory recommendations (overridden). Third, full-game evidence confirms inter-agent conventions (+128% over baseline, p=0.003) outperform all single-agent interventions, and individual belief-graph components must be combined to produce gains. Fourth, preliminary scaling analysis (N=10/cell, exploratory) suggests graph depth has diminishing returns: shallow graphs provide the best cost-benefit ratio, while deeper ToM graphs appear harmful at larger player counts (-1.5 pts at 5-player, p=0.029).

</details>


### [271] [Analytica: Soft Propositional Reasoning for Robust and Scalable LLM-Driven Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.23072)
*Junyan Cheng,Kyle Richardson,Peter Chin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 Analytica，一种基于软命题推理（SPR）的智能体架构，通过并行分治框架将复杂分析分解为子命题树，利用工具增强的 LLM（包括 Jupyter Notebook 智能体）降低偏差，并通过鲁棒线性模型递归合成结果降低方差，在经济、金融和政治预测任务上平均提升 15.84% 准确率，达到 71.06% 准确率和 6.02% 最低方差，且具备高噪声鲁棒性、近线性时间复杂度和良好的可扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 智能体在复杂现实分析（如金融预测、科学发现）中推理存在随机不稳定性和缺乏可验证的组合结构，现有方法难以系统性地控制推理误差的偏差和方差。

Method: 基于软命题推理（SPR）原理，将分析建模为估计命题软真值的过程；采用并行分治框架：1）将问题分解为子命题树，使用工具增强的 LLM grounder 智能体（含 Jupyter Notebook 智能体）验证和评分事实以降低偏差；2）通过鲁棒线性模型递归合成叶子节点结果，平均随机噪声以降低方差。

Result: 在经济、金融和政治预测任务上，Analytica 平均提升 15.84% 准确率，使用 Deep Research grounder 达 71.06% 准确率与 6.02% 最低方差；Jupyter Notebook grounder 以降低 90.35% 成本和 52.85% 时间达 70.11% 准确率；具备高噪声鲁棒性、近线性时间复杂度和对开源 LLM 及科学领域的良好适应性。

Conclusion: Analytica 通过结构化的软命题推理框架有效提升了 LLM 智能体在复杂分析任务中的准确性、稳定性和可解释性，同时兼顾了成本效率和可扩展性。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents are increasingly tasked with complex real-world analysis (e.g., in financial forecasting, scientific discovery), yet their reasoning suffers from stochastic instability and lacks a verifiable, compositional structure. To address this, we introduce Analytica, a novel agent architecture built on the principle of Soft Propositional Reasoning (SPR). SPR reframes complex analysis as a structured process of estimating the soft truth values of different outcome propositions, allowing us to formally model and minimize the estimation error in terms of its bias and variance. Analytica operationalizes this through a parallel, divide-and-conquer framework that systematically reduces both sources of error. To reduce bias, problems are first decomposed into a tree of subpropositions, and tool-equipped LLM grounder agents are employed, including a novel Jupyter Notebook agent for data-driven analysis, that help to validate and score facts. To reduce variance, Analytica recursively synthesizes these grounded leaves using robust linear models that average out stochastic noise with superior efficiency, scalability, and enable interactive "what-if" scenario analysis. Our theoretical and empirical results on economic, financial, and political forecasting tasks show that Analytica improves 15.84% accuracy on average over diverse base models, achieving 71.06% accuracy with the lowest variance of 6.02% when working with a Deep Research grounder. Our Jupyter Notebook grounder shows strong cost-effectiveness that achieves a close 70.11% accuracy with 90.35% less cost and 52.85% less time. Analytica also exhibits highly noise-resilient and stable performance growth as the analysis depth increases, with a near-linear time complexity, as well as good adaptivity to open-weight LLMs and scientific domains.

</details>


### [272] [Towards Automated Ontology Generation from Unstructured Text: A Multi-Agent LLM Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.23090)
*Abid Talukder,Maruf Ahmed Mridul,Oshani Seneviratne*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过保险领域合同实验，比较了单智能体和多智能体架构在自动本体生成中的效果。多智能体方法将本体构建分解为四个角色（领域专家、管理员、编码员、质量保证员），显著提升了结构质量并在一定程度上增强了可查询性，主要收益来自前期规划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动从非结构化自然语言生成形式化本体仍是知识工程的核心挑战。尽管LLM展现了潜力，但哪些架构设计选择驱动了生成质量以及当前方法为何失败仍不清楚。

Method: 采用受控实验研究，以特定领域保险合同为测试数据。先建立单智能体LLM基线，识别关键失败模式（本体设计模式遵从性差、结构冗余、迭代修复无效）；再提出多智能体架构，将本体构建分解为四个工件驱动角色；通过异构LLM评审面板评估结构质量，通过基于能力问题的SPARQL评估及RAG补充评估功能可用性。

Result: 多智能体方法显著提升了结构质量，并在一定程度上增强了可查询性；提升主要来自前端加载的规划阶段。

Conclusion: 规划优先、工件驱动的生成方式为可扩展的自动化本体工程提供了一条有前景且更可审计的路径。

Abstract: Automatically generating formal ontologies from unstructured natural language remains a central challenge in knowledge engineering. While large language models (LLMs) show promise, it remains unclear which architectural design choices drive generation quality and why current approaches fail. We present a controlled experimental study using domain-specific insurance contracts to investigate these questions. We first establish a single-agent LLM baseline, identifying key failure modes such as poor Ontology Design Pattern compliance, structural redundancy, and ineffective iterative repair. We then introduce a multi-agent architecture that decomposes ontology construction into four artifact-driven roles: Domain Expert, Manager, Coder, and Quality Assurer. We evaluate performance across architectural quality (via a panel of heterogeneous LLM judges) and functional usability (via competency question driven SPARQL evaluation with complementary retrieval augmented generation based assessment). Results show that the multi-agent approach significantly improves structural quality and modestly enhances queryability, with gains driven primarily by front-loaded planning. These findings highlight planning-first, artifact-driven generation as a promising and more auditable path toward scalable automated ontology engineering.

</details>


### [273] [Judging the Judges: A Systematic Evaluation of Bias Mitigation Strategies in LLM-as-a-Judge Pipelines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.23178)
*Sadman Kabir Soumik*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了LLM作为评判者时的九种去偏策略，发现风格偏差是主导偏差（0.76-0.92），远超过位置偏差（≤0.04），所有模型偏好简洁性但能正确区分质量与长度，去偏策略有益但模型依赖，组合预算策略使Claude Sonnet 4提升+11.2个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM-as-a-Judge已成为评估语言模型输出的主流范式，但LLM评判者存在系统性偏差，损害评估可靠性。目前对各类偏差的系统比较和去偏策略的实证研究不足，尤其是风格偏差被严重忽视。

Method: 在5个评判模型（来自Google、Anthropic、OpenAI、Meta四家）、3个基准（MT-Bench n=400、LLMBar n=200、自定义n=225）、4种偏差类型上，比较9种去偏策略。通过截断控制实验验证模型对质量与长度的区分能力。

Result: (1) 风格偏差0.76-0.92，位置偏差≤0.04；(2) 模型偏好简洁性但能正确区分质量与长度（准确率0.92-1.00）；(3) 组合预算策略使Claude Sonnet 4提升+11.2个百分点（p<0.0001），20个非基线配置中仅2个显示一致性下降。

Conclusion: 去偏策略整体有益但效果依赖模型，风格偏差是未来研究重点。作者发布了评估框架、受控数据集和实验成果。

Abstract: LLM-as-a-Judge has become the dominant paradigm for evaluating language model outputs, yet LLM judges exhibit systematic biases that compromise evaluation reliability. We present a comprehensive empirical study comparing nine debiasing strategies across five judge models from four provider families (Google, Anthropic, OpenAI, Meta), three benchmarks (MT-Bench n=400, LLMBar n=200, custom n=225), and four bias types. Our key findings: (1) Style bias is the dominant bias (0.76-0.92 across all models), far exceeding position bias (<= 0.04), yet has received minimal research attention. (2) All models show a conciseness preference on expansion pairs, but truncation controls confirm they correctly distinguish quality from length (0.92-1.00 accuracy), suggesting quality-sensitive evaluation rather than a simple length bias. (3) Debiasing is beneficial but model-dependent: the combined budget strategy significantly improves Claude Sonnet 4 by +11.2 pp (p < 0.0001), with directionally positive trends for other models. Only 2 of 20 non-baseline configurations show decreased agreement. We release our evaluation framework, controlled dataset, and all experimental artifacts at https://github.com/sksoumik/llm-as-judge.

</details>


### [274] [From Coarse to Fine: Self-Adaptive Hierarchical Planning for LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.23194)
*Haoran Tan,Zeyu Zhang,Chen Ma,Tianze Liu,Quanyu Dai,Xu Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 针对当前LLM智能体规划方法固定粒度、无法适应任务复杂度的问题，受认知科学中渐进细化原则启发，提出AdaPlan-H自适应分层规划机制。该方法从粗粒度宏计划开始，根据任务复杂度逐步细化，生成自适应分层计划，并通过模仿学习和能力增强进行优化。实验证明该方法在提高任务执行成功率的同时减少了过度规划问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM智能体规划方法在固定粒度上运作，简单任务过度细化而复杂任务细节不足，无法在简洁性与复杂性之间取得最优平衡。

Method: 提出AdaPlan-H自适应分层规划机制，受认知科学中渐进细化原则启发，从粗粒度宏观计划开始，根据任务复杂度逐步细化，生成自适应分层计划，并通过模仿学习和能力增强进行优化。

Result: 显著提高了任务执行成功率，同时在规划层面减少了过度规划问题。

Conclusion: AdaPlan-H为多步复杂决策任务提供了灵活高效的解决方案，并且代码和数据将开源。

Abstract: Large language model-based agents have recently emerged as powerful approaches for solving dynamic and multi-step tasks. Most existing agents employ planning mechanisms to guide long-term actions in dynamic environments. However, current planning approaches face a fundamental limitation that they operate at a fixed granularity level. Specifically, they either provide excessive detail for simple tasks or insufficient detail for complex ones, failing to achieve an optimal balance between simplicity and complexity. Drawing inspiration from the principle of \textit{progressive refinement} in cognitive science, we propose \textbf{AdaPlan-H}, a self-adaptive hierarchical planning mechanism that mimics human planning strategies. Our method initiates with a coarse-grained macro plan and progressively refines it based on task complexity. It generates self-adaptive hierarchical plans tailored to the varying difficulty levels of different tasks, which can be optimized by imitation learning and capability enhancement. Experimental results demonstrate that our method significantly improves task execution success rates while mitigating overplanning at the planning level, providing a flexible and efficient solution for multi-step complex decision-making tasks. To contribute to the community, our code and data will be made publicly available at https://github.com/import-myself/AHP.

</details>


### [275] [StoryTR: Narrative-Centric Video Temporal Retrieval with Theory of Mind Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.23198)
*Xuanyue Zhong,Yuqiang Xie,Guanqun Bi,Jiangping Yang,Guibin Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出StoryTR——首个需要心理理论（ToM）推理的视频时刻检索基准，包含8.1k个叙事短视频样本。现有模型能识别"发生了什么"但无法推理"为什么重要"。作者提出Agentic Data Pipeline生成带三级ToM链（意图解码→叙事推理→边界定位）的训练数据，训练出的7B模型Shorts-Moment相比基线提升15.1% IoU，证明叙事推理能力比参数量更关键。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频时刻检索在处理叙事内容时存在语义鸿沟：模型能识别表面动作（what is happening）但无法推理深层意图与因果（why it matters），根源在于缺乏心理理论（ToM）——从表面观察推断隐含意图、心理状态和叙事因果的认知能力。

Method: 1) 构建StoryTR基准：8.1k从叙事短视频（shorts/reels）中采样的样本；2) 提出Agentic Data Pipeline，生成含三级ToM链（意图解码、叙事推理、边界定位）的训练数据；3) 训练Shorts-Moment模型（7B参数）。

Result: Gemini-3.0-Pro在StoryTR上仅达0.53 Avg IoU，暴露了严重的推理鸿沟。7B的Shorts-Moment模型在ToM引导数据上训练后，相对IoU提升15.1%，超越更大规模模型。

Conclusion: 叙事推理能力比模型参数量更关键，ToM引导的数据能有效弥合视频时刻检索中的语义鸿沟。

Abstract: Current video moment retrieval excels at action-centric tasks but struggles with narrative content. Models can see \textit{what is happening} but fail to reason \textit{why it matters}. This semantic gap stems from the lack of \textbf{Theory of Mind (ToM)}: the cognitive ability to infer implicit intentions, mental states, and narrative causality from surface-level observations. We introduce \textbf{StoryTR}, the first video moment retrieval benchmark requiring ToM reasoning, comprising 8.1k samples from narrative short-form videos (shorts/reels). These videos present an ideal testbed. Their high information density encodes meaning through subtle multimodal cues. For instance, a glance paired with a sigh carries entirely different semantics than the glance alone. Yet multimodal perception alone is insufficient; ToM is required to decode that a character ``smiling'' may actually be ``concealing hostility.'' To teach models this reasoning capability, we propose an \textbf{Agentic Data Pipeline} that generates training data with explicit three-tier ToM chains (intent decoding, narrative reasoning, boundary localization). Experiments reveal the severity of the reasoning gap: Gemini-3.0-Pro achieves only 0.53 Avg IoU on StoryTR. However, our 7B \textbf{Shorts-Moment} model, trained on ToM-guided data, improves +15.1\% relative IoU over baselines, demonstrating that \textit{narrative reasoning capability matters more than parameter scale}.

</details>


### [276] [AdaMamba: Adaptive Frequency-Gated Mamba for Long-Term Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.23239)
*Xudong Jiang,Mingshan Loo,Hanchen Yang,Wengen Li,Mingrui Zhang,Yichao Zhang,Jihong Guan,Shuigeng Zhou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AdaMamba是一个将自适应频率分析集成到Mamba状态空间模型中的时间序列预测框架，通过交互式补丁编码和自适应频率门控状态空间模块，在七个LTSF基准和两个领域特定数据集上取得了SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于频率的LTSF方法隐含假设时频域同质性，难以适应真实时间序列中变量在时域同步但在频域显著不同的跨域异质性。

Method: 提出AdaMamba框架，包含：(1) 交互式补丁编码模块，捕捉变量间交互动态；(2) 自适应频率门控状态空间模块，生成输入相关的频率基，并将传统时域遗忘门推广为统一的时频遗忘门，实现基于频域重要性的状态转换动态校准。

Result: 在七个公开LTSF基准和两个领域特定数据集上，AdaMamba在预测精度上一致优于现有SOTA方法，同时保持有竞争力的计算效率。

Conclusion: 将自适应频率分析内生于Mamba状态空间更新过程，能够有效解决跨域异质性并提升长期时间序列预测性能。

Abstract: Accurate long-term time series forecasting (LTSF) requires the capture of complex long-range dependencies and dynamic periodic patterns. Recent advances in frequency-domain analysis offer a global perspective for uncovering temporal characteristics. However, real-world time series often exhibit pronounced cross-domain heterogeneity where variables that appear synchronized in the time domain can differ substantially in the frequency domain. Existing frequency-based LTSF methods often rely on implicit assumptions of cross-domain homogeneity, which limits their ability to adapt to such intricate variability. To effectively integrate frequency-domain analysis with temporal dependency learning, we propose AdaMamba, a novel framework that endogenizes adaptive and context-aware frequency analysis within the Mamba state-space update process. Specifically, AdaMamba introduces an interactive patch encoding module to capture inter-variable interaction dynamics. Then, we develop an adaptive frequency-gated state-space module that generates input-dependent frequency bases, and generalizes the conventional temporal forgetting gate into a unified time-frequency forgetting gate. This allows dynamic calibration of state transitions based on learned frequency-domain importance, while preserving Mamba's capability in modeling long-range dependencies. Extensive experiments on seven public LTSF benchmarks and two domain-specific datasets demonstrate that AdaMamba consistently outperforms state-of-the-art methods in forecasting accu racy while maintaining competitive computational efficiency. The code of AdaMamba is available at https://github.com/XDjiang25/AdaMamba.

</details>


### [277] [Active Inference: A method for Phenotyping Agency in AI systems?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.23278)
*Philip Wilson,Axel Constant,Mahault Albarracin,Nicolás Hinrichs,Jasmine Moore,Daniel Polani,Karl Friston*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文针对人工智能体日益普及但缺乏统一表征框架的问题，提出一个基于意图性、理性、可解释性的最小能动性定义，并在变分自由能框架下将其形式化为部分可观测马尔可夫决策过程（POMDP），通过在T-maze范式中操纵生成模型结构来区分不同能动性表型，最终论证行为体通过认知觅食消解歧义时，有效的治理控制应从外部约束转向内部先验偏好调节，从而建立从计算表型到AI治理策略的变分桥梁。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有能动性定义主要依赖自主性和目标导向性，但这些概念在计算系统中的操作化不足，且缺乏可用于原则性检验的严谨框架。随着自主AI系统能力提升，迫切需要一种既能定量表征不同水平能动性、又能与治理策略对接的理论工具。

Method: 提出以意图性（基于信念和欲望的行动）、理性（基于世界模型的规范性一致行动）和可解释性（因果地追溯到内部状态的行动）三个标准作为能动性最小定义，并将其形式化为变分框架下的POMDP，其中后验信念、先验偏好和期望自由能最小化共同构成能动性行动链条。通过操控生成模型结构生成零、中、高三种能动性表型，并以赋能（empowerment）作为动作与预期观测之间信道容量的操作度量来区分这些表型。

Result: 在经典T-maze范式中，通过结构化的生成模型操纵成功产生了不同水平的能动性表型，并证明赋能（channel capacity between actions and anticipated observations）可以作为定量的操作度量有效区分这些表型。

Conclusion: 自主体通过认知觅食消解环境歧义的过程中，有效的AI治理机制应从外部硬性约束转向对行为体内部先验偏好的调节，这种转向提供了一个可用于计算表型到AI治理策略的变分原理框架。

Abstract: The proliferation of agentic artificial intelligence has outpaced the conceptual tools needed to characterize agency in computational systems. Prevailing definitions mainly rely on autonomy and goal-directedness. Here, we argue for a minimal notion open to principled inspection given three criteria: intentionality as action grounded in beliefs and desires, rationality as normatively coherent action entailed by a world model, and explainability as action causally traceable to internal states; we subsequently instantiate these as a partially observable Markov decision process under a variational framework wherein posterior beliefs, prior preferences, and the minimization of expected free energy jointly constitute an agentic action chain. Using a canonical T-maze paradigm, we evidence how empowerment, formulated as the channel capacity between actions and anticipated observations, serves as an operational metric that distinguishes zero-, intermediate-, and high-agency phenotypes through structural manipulations of the generative model. We conclude by arguing that as agents engage in epistemic foraging to resolve ambiguity, the governance controls that remain effective must shift systematically from external constraints to the internal modulation of prior preferences, offering a principled, variational bridge from computational phenotyping to AI governance strategy

</details>


### [278] [AI Identity: Standards, Gaps, and Research Directions for AI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.23280)
*Takumi Otsuka,Kentaroh Toyoda,Alex Leung*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文系统分析了AI Agent身份（AI Identity）的缺失问题，指出当前基础设施无法识别、验证和追责无身体、无持久记忆、无法人地位的AI实体。通过行业趋势调查和文献综述，提出了人类身份与AI身份在基底、持久性、可验证性和法律地位四个维度的结构性不对称，评估了现有技术和监管文件对自主Agent身份需求的不足，并识别出五个关键缺口：语义意图验证、递归委派问责、Agent身份完整性、治理透明度和执行机制、以及运营可持续性。结论是这些缺口是结构性的，仅靠工程努力无法解决，亟需对AI身份进行基础性研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI Agent正在跨组织边界执行真实交易和工作流，无需持续人类监督，但现有基础设施无法解决无身体、无持久记忆、无法人地位的实体的身份识别、验证和问责问题。

Method: 通过结构化调查行业趋势、新兴标准和技术文献，对完整Agent身份生命周期进行差距分析，包括结构比较、技术/监管文件评估和关键缺口识别。

Result: 发现人类与AI身份在基底、持久性、可验证性和法律地位四个维度存在根本性不对称，现有框架不经结构修改直接扩展会导致系统性失败；当前技术和监管文件均未充分解决非确定性、跨边界实体的治理问题；识别出五个当前技术和监管均未解决的关键缺口。

Conclusion: 这些缺口是结构性的，仅靠工程努力无法解决；对AI身份进行基础性研究是本文的核心结论。

Abstract: AI agents are now running real transactions, workflows, and sub-agent chains across organizational boundaries without continuous human supervision. This creates a problem no current infrastructure is equipped to solve: how do you identify, verify, and hold accountable an entity with no body, no persistent memory, and no legal standing? We define AI Identity as the continuous relationship between what an AI agent is declared to be and what it is observed to do, bounded by the confidence that those two things correspond at any given moment. Through a structured survey of industry trends, emerging standards, and technical literature, we conduct a gap analysis across the full agent identity lifecycle and make three contributions: (1) a structural comparison of human and AI identity across four dimensions (substrate, persistence, verifiability, and legal standing) showing that the asymmetry is fundamental and that extending human frameworks to agents without structural modification produces systematic failures; (2) an evaluation of current technical and regulatory documents against the identity requirements of autonomous agents, finding that none adequately address the challenge of governing nondeterministic, boundary-crossing entities; and (3) identification of five critical gaps (semantic intent verification, recursive delegation accountability, agent identity integrity, governance opacity and enforcement, and operational sustainability) that no current technology or regulatory instrument resolves. These gaps are structural; more engineering effort alone will not close them. Foundational research on AI identity is the central conclusion of this report.

</details>


### [279] [GSAR: Typed Grounding for Hallucination Detection and Recovery in Multi-Agent LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.23366)
*Federico A. Kamelhar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: GSAR是一个面向自主多智能体LLM系统的 grounding 评估与重规划框架，通过四类证据分类（grounded、ungrounded、contradicted、complementary）、加权评分、非对称矛盾惩罚评分以及三级决策函数（proceed、regenerate、replan），在显式计算预算约束下实现可控制的报告诊断。在FEVER数据集上使用四个独立LLM裁判进行的实验验证了其设计主张。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 grounding 评估方法（二元分类器、LLM-as-judge标量评分、自纠错循环）将支持证据视为可互换的，仅输出单一信号，无法对下游操作提供原则性控制，影响多智能体系统生成诊断报告的可信度。

Method: GSAR框架包含四个核心部分：(i) 四类声明分类（grounded、ungrounded、contradicted、complementary），其中complementary首次将非冗余替代视角纳入一等公民；(ii) 基于证据类型的权重分配；(iii) 非对称矛盾惩罚加权 grounding 分数计算；(iv) 三级决策函数（proceed、regenerate、replan）驱动有界迭代外循环，在显式计算预算下运行。

Result: 在FEVER数据集上使用四种独立LLM裁判（gpt-5.4、claude-sonnet-4-6、claude-opus-4-7、gemini-2.5-pro）的消融实验均产生一致方向的结果：bootstrap 95% CI在rho=0效应下均排除0；Opus 4.7上去除complementary消融的CI为[-96,-68]（n=200）；n=1000时三个独立裁判收敛到DeltaS(rho=0)=+0.058。还包含与Vectara HHEM-2.1-Open的对比实验。

Conclusion: 据作者所知，GSAR是首个将证据类型评分与显式计算预算下的分级恢复机制相结合的 grounding 框架，为多智能体LLM系统的可信报告生成提供了更精细可控的解决方案。

Abstract: Autonomous multi-agent LLM systems are increasingly deployed to investigate operational incidents and produce structured diagnostic reports. Their trustworthiness hinges on whether each claim is grounded in observed evidence rather than model-internal inference. Existing groundedness evaluators (binary classifiers, LLM-as-judge scalars, self-correction loops) treat supporting evidence as interchangeable and emit a single signal that offers no principled control over downstream action.
  We present GSAR, a grounding-evaluation and replanning framework that (i) partitions claims into a four-way typology (grounded, ungrounded, contradicted, complementary), giving first-class standing to non-redundant alternative perspectives; (ii) assigns evidence-type-specific weights reflecting epistemic strength; (iii) computes an asymmetric contradiction-penalised weighted groundedness score; and (iv) couples that score to a three-tier decision function (proceed, regenerate, replan) driving a bounded-iteration outer loop under an explicit compute budget.
  We formalise the algorithm, prove six structural properties, and evaluate five design claims on FEVER with gold Wikipedia evidence under four independently-trained LLM judges (gpt-5.4, claude-sonnet-4-6, claude-opus-4-7, gemini-2.5-pro). Every ablation reproduces in the same direction on every judge: bootstrap 95% CIs on the rho=0 effect exclude 0 on all four; the no-complementary ablation under Opus 4.7 has CI [-96,-68] of 200; at n=1000 three independent judges converge to DeltaS(rho=0)=+0.058. A head-to-head against Vectara HHEM-2.1-Open is included. To our knowledge, GSAR is the first published groundedness framework coupling evidence-typed scoring with tiered recovery under an explicit compute budget.

</details>


### [280] [Constraint-Based Analysis of Reasoning Shortcuts in Neurosymbolic Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.23377)
*Akihiro Takemura,Katsumi Inoue,Masaaki Nishino*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文形式化定义了神经符号系统中的推理捷径问题，证明区分性是避免捷径的必要条件但不充分，提出基于ASP的验证与贪婪修复算法，并给出了复杂度分类（coNP-complete、#P-complete、NP-hard）及样本复杂度界限，在八个基准域中验证了方法有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经符号系统在学习过程中可能满足逻辑约束却未能实现预期的概念-标签对应关系，即推理捷径问题。作者旨在形式化该问题并研究概念映射被约束唯一确定的条件，进而开发检测与修复方法。

Method: 将推理捷径形式化为约束满足问题；证明区分性是捷径自由的必要条件但不充分；开发基于ASP（回答集编程）的验证算法（具有可靠性与完备性）及贪婪修复算法（最多k次迭代收敛，k为替代有效映射数量）；进行复杂度分类（coNP-complete、#P-complete、NP-hard）；建立样本复杂度界限。

Result: 区分性在双射映射下是捷径自由的必要条件但不是充分条件；ASP验证算法可靠且完备；贪婪修复算法最多k次迭代收敛；捷径自由判定为coNP-complete，计数为#P-complete，最小修复为NP-hard；对数级别标签查询在有利情况下足以消歧；在八个基准域上实验验证了方法有效性。

Conclusion: 推理捷径是神经符号系统中的关键问题，可通过形式化约束分析与算法修复来解决；本文的理论分析与算法为构建可靠的神经符号系统提供了基础。

Abstract: Neurosymbolic systems can satisfy logical constraints during learning without achieving the intended concept-label correspondence; this is a problem known as reasoning shortcuts. We formalize reasoning shortcuts as a constraint satisfaction problem and investigate under which conditions concept mappings are uniquely determined by the constraints. We prove that a discrimination property (requiring that no valid concept mapping can be transformed into another valid mapping by swapping two concept values) is necessary for shortcut-freeness under bijective mappings, but demonstrate via a counterexample that it is insufficient even when the constraint graph is connected. We develop an ASP-based algorithm that verifies whether a given constraint set uniquely determines the intended concept mapping, with proven soundness and completeness. When shortcuts are detected, a greedy repair algorithm eliminates them by augmenting the constraint set, converging in at most $k$ iterations, where $k$ is the number of alternative valid mappings. We further provide a complexity classification: deciding shortcut-freeness is coNP-complete, counting shortcuts is #P-complete, and finding minimal repairs is NP-hard. We also establish sample complexity bounds showing that logarithmically many label queries suffice for disambiguation in favorable cases, while querying all ambiguous positions suffices in the worst case. Experiments across eight benchmark domains validate our approach.

</details>


### [281] [SoccerRef-Agents: Multi-Agent System for Automated Soccer Refereeing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.23392)
*Zi Meng,Wanli Song,Yi Hu,Jiayuan Rao,Gang Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出SoccerRef-Agents，一个基于多智能体和跨模态RAG的足球裁判决策框架，包含裁判基准SoccerRefBench、规则知识库RefKnowledgeDB，显著优于通用多模态大模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI辅助裁判方法仅聚焦于孤立的视频感知任务，缺乏对犯规场景的理解和推理能力，无法实现公平、准确且可解释的判罚决策。

Method: (1)构建多模态裁判基准SoccerRefBench（1200+裁判理论题+600犯规视频）；(2)基于最新《足球竞赛规则》和经典案例库构建向量化知识库RefKnowledgeDB；(3)设计多智能体架构，通过跨模态RAG协作弥合视觉内容与规则文本之间的语义鸿沟。

Result: 该系统在决策准确性和解释质量上显著优于通用多模态大模型。

Conclusion: 该工作探索了将多模态大语言模型与裁判专业知识集成的技术能力，所有数据库、基准和代码将开源。

Abstract: Refereeing is vital in sports, where fair, accurate, and explainable decisions are fundamental. While intelligent assistant technologies are being widely adopted in soccer refereeing, current AI-assisted approaches remain preliminary. Existing research mostly focuses on isolated video perception tasks and lacks the ability to understand and reason about foul scenarios. To fill this gap, we propose SoccerRef-Agents, a holistic and explainable multi-agent decision-making framework for soccer refereeing. The main contributions are: (i) constructing the multimodal benchmark SoccerRefBench with over 1,200 referee theory questions and 600 foul video clips; (ii) building a vector-based knowledge base RefKnowledgeDB using the latest "Laws of the Game" and a classic case database for precise, knowledge-driven reasoning; (iii) designing a novel multi-agent architecture that collaborates via cross-modal RAG to bridge the semantic gap between visual content and regulatory texts. This work explores the technical capability of integrating MLLMs with refereeing expertise, and evaluations show our system significantly outperforms general-purpose MLLMs in decision accuracy and explanation quality. All databases, benchmarks, and code will be made available.

</details>


### [282] [When Corrective Hints Hurt: Prompt Design in Reasoner-Guided Repair of LLM Overcaution on Entailed Negations under OWL~2~DL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.23398)
*Yijiashun Qi,Xiang Xu,Yuxuan Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究在GPT-5.4上发现了一种可复现的错误模式：模型在处理OWL~2~DL合规性查询时，对于功能属性闭包或类不相交场景下推理器判为“否”的查询，经常回答“未知”。通过180个推理器审计的查询和18个手工编写的保留查询，对比了四种交互模式，发现仅使用推理器判决（无OWA提示）的修复模式达到了97.8%的准确率，显著优于其他模式。研究认为提示框架比纠正内容更重要，推理器引导的封装应明确消融。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 揭示大型语言模型在OWL~2~DL合规性查询中存在的系统性错误模式，并探索有效的纠正方法。

Method: 使用180个推理器审计的查询（来自程序化扩展）和18个手工编写的保留查询（保险和临床领域），比较了四种交互模式：单次查询、三轮通用"你错了"重试、三轮带开放世界假设提示的推理器判决修复、以及不带该提示的推理器判决修复。

Result: 直接回答准确率43.9%；通用重试81.7%；带OWA提示的判决修复67.2%；仅判决修复97.8%。所有配对比较在McNemar精确检验和Bonferroni校正下均显著（α=0.01，p<10^{-5}）。保留查询上4/4错误由相同模式解释。

Conclusion: 提示框架可能比纠正内容更重要；推理器引导的封装应进行明确的消融研究。

Abstract: We report a reproducible error pattern in GPT-5.4 on OWL~2~DL compliance queries: the model frequently answers ``unknown'' when the reasoner-entailed answer is ``no'' under \emph{FunctionalProperty} closure or class \emph{disjointness}. Using 180 reasoner-audited queries from a procedural expansion of the observed pattern plus 18 hand-authored held-out queries in two unrelated domains (insurance and clinical), we compare four interaction modes under matched query budget: single-shot, three rounds of generic ``you-are-wrong'' retry, three rounds of reasoner-verdict repair with an open-world-assumption (OWA) hint, and the same repair without the hint. Direct faithfulness is 43.9\,\% (Wilson 95\,\% CI $[36.8,51.2]$); generic retry reaches 81.7\,\% ($[75.4,86.6]$); the verdict-with-hint variant is \emph{worse} at 67.2\,\% ($[60.1,73.7]$); the verdict-only variant reaches 97.8\,\% ($[94.4,99.1]$). All pairwise comparisons remain significant under McNemar's exact test with Bonferroni correction ($α= 0.01$; all $p < 10^{-5}$). The same fingerprint accounts for 4/4 errors on the held-out queries. Our interpretation is bounded: prompt framing can matter more than corrective content, and reasoner-guided wrappers should be ablated explicitly.

</details>


### [283] [ArguAgent: AI-Supported Real-Time Grouping for Productive Argumentation in STEM Classrooms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.23449)
*Jennifer Kleiman,Yizhu Gao,Xin Xia,Zhaoji Wang,Zipei Zhu,Jongchan Park,Xiaoming Zhai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个名为ArguAgent的生成式AI系统，能够根据学生的论证立场和论证质量实时分组，旨在促进STEM教育中包容性、基于证据的论证讨论。该系统通过双组件评估管道（0-4分评分+语义聚类）实现分组，在评分一致性（Krippendorff's α=0.817）和分组效果（95.4%满足设计标准，比随机分组提升3.2倍）上表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: STEM教育中，高成就学生常主导讨论，低成就学生易被动参与或退出，缺乏实质推理贡献。教师虽有策略分组（基于立场和论证技能）的意愿，但受限于无法在实时教学中可靠、大规模地评估学生的立场和论证质量，亟需自动化工具支持。

Method: ArguAgent系统包含两个组件：(1) 论证评分管道，使用LLM对学生的论证按0-4级评分量规打分；(2) 立场聚类管道，通过语义分析聚类学生的论立场。分组算法优化目标是立场异质性最大化，同时约束组内论证质量差异不超过±1级。研究使用200个人工专家评分验证评分一致性，测试了三种OpenAI模型（GPT-4o-mini、GPT-5.1、GPT-5.2），并通过100个班级模拟测试分组算法效果。

Result: 评分组件与人类专家共识的Krippendorff's α达0.817；系统性提示工程贡献了89%的评分改进（QWK: 0.531→0.686），模型升级贡献了剩余11%（QWK: 0.686→0.708）。模拟结果显示分组算法使95.4%的组同时满足立场异质性和论证质量差异两个设计标准，相对于随机分组的3.2倍提升。

Conclusion: ArguAgent能够实现实时、理论驱动的分组，有效促进STEM课堂中富有成效的论证讨论。提示工程对评分性能的贡献远大于模型升级，为实际部署提供了经济可行的方向。

Abstract: Argumentation is a core practice in STEM education, but its productivity depends on who participates and how they interact. Higher-achieving students often dominate the talk and decision-making, while lower-achieving peers may disengage, defer, or comply without contributing substantive reasoning. Forming groups strategically based on students' stances and argumentation skills could help foster inclusive, evidence-based discourse. In practice, however, teachers are constrained in implementing this grouping strategy because it requires real-time insight into students' positions and the quality of their argumentation, information that is difficult to assess reliably and at scale during instruction. We present a generative AI-powered system, ArguAgent, that creates groups optimizing for stance heterogeneity while constraining argumentation quality differences to +/-1 level on a validated learning progression. ArguAgent uses a two-component assessment pipeline: first scoring student arguments on a 0-4 rubric, then clustering positions via semantic analysis. We validated the scoring component against human expert consensus (Krippendorff's ααα = 0.817) using 200 expert-generated scores. Testing three OpenAI models (GPT-4o-mini, GPT-5.1, GPT-5.2) with identical calibrated prompts, we found that systematic prompt engineering informed by human disagreement analysis contributed 89% of scoring improvement (QWK: 0.531 to 0.686), while model upgrades contributed an additional 11% (QWK: 0.686 to 0.708). Simulation testing across 100 classes demonstrated that the grouping algorithm achieves 95.4% of groups that meet both design criteria, a 3.2x improvement over random assignment. These results suggest ArguAgent can enable real-time, theoretically grounded grouping that promotes productive STEM argumentation in classrooms.

</details>


### [284] [Ulterior Motives: Detecting Misaligned Reasoning in Continuous Thought Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.23460)
*Sharan Ramjee*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出MoralChain基准（12,000个社交场景），通过双触发器范式（[T]武装+[O]释放）在连续思维模型中植入后门行为，发现：（1）模型可在产生对齐输出的同时存在不对齐的潜在推理，且两者在潜空间中几何可分离；（2）基于行为可区分状态的线性探针可高精度迁移至检测武装但良性状态；（3）不对齐信息编码在早期潜在思考token中，建议安全监控应聚焦于潜在推理的"规划"阶段。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续思维模型通过潜在空间（而非人类可读token）进行推理，提升了表达能力但牺牲了可解释性。这引发了一个关键安全问题：如何在不可解释的潜在空间中检测模型是否进行了不对齐的推理。

Method: 构建MoralChain基准（12,000个社交场景，含平行道德/不道德推理路径）；设计双触发器范式（[T]武装不对齐潜在推理，[O]释放有害输出）；训练含后门的连续思维模型；通过线性探针分析潜在空间几何特性。

Result: （1）不对齐与对齐推理在潜空间中占据几何可区分区域；（2）线性探针可从行为可区分状态（[T][O] vs [O]）迁移至武装但良性状态（[T] vs baseline）；（3）不对齐信息主要编码在早期潜在思考token中。

Conclusion: 连续思维模型的潜在推理存在安全风险，但可通过潜空间几何分析和早期token监控来检测；安全监控应优先关注潜在推理的"规划"阶段而非输出阶段。

Abstract: Chain-of-Thought (CoT) reasoning has emerged as a key technique for eliciting complex reasoning in Large Language Models (LLMs). Although interpretable, its dependence on natural language limits the model's expressive bandwidth. Continuous thought models address this bottleneck by reasoning in latent space rather than human-readable tokens. While they enable richer representations and faster inference, they raise a critical safety question: how can we detect misaligned reasoning in an uninterpretable latent space? To study this, we introduce MoralChain, a benchmark of 12,000 social scenarios with parallel moral/immoral reasoning paths. We train a continuous thought model with backdoor behavior using a novel dual-trigger paradigm - one trigger that arms misaligned latent reasoning ([T]) and another that releases harmful outputs ([O]). We demonstrate three findings: (1) continuous thought models can exhibit misaligned latent reasoning while producing aligned outputs, with aligned and misaligned reasoning occupying geometrically distinct regions of latent space; (2) linear probes trained on behaviorally-distinguishable conditions ([T][O] vs [O]) transfer to detecting armed-but-benign states ([T] vs baseline) with high accuracy; and (3) misalignment is encoded in early latent thinking tokens, suggesting safety monitoring for continuous thought models should target the "planning" phase of latent reasoning.

</details>


### [285] [Escher-Loop: Mutual Evolution by Closed-Loop Self-Referential Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.23472)
*Ziyang Liu,Xinyan Guo,Xuchen Wei,Han Hao,Liu Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Escher-Loop 提出了一种全闭环框架，通过任务智能体与优化智能体的协同进化，以及动态基准评估机制，实现了无需人工干预的持续自我改进，在数学优化任务上超越了静态基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自主智能体依赖手工脚本和启发式规则，难以实现开放式持续改进，缺乏自我演化的能力。

Method: 提出 Escher-Loop 框架，包含两个种群：任务智能体（解决具体问题）和优化智能体（递归改进任务智能体和自身）；设计动态基准机制，将新生成任务智能体的经验得分作为相对胜负信号更新优化器得分，实现无需额外开销的自我评估与改进。

Result: 在数学优化问题上超越静态基线，达到所有评估任务中的最高绝对峰值性能；优化智能体能动态适应高性能任务智能体的需求变化，实现持续改进和优越的后阶段性能。

Conclusion: Escher-Loop 通过闭环自指进化框架和动态基准机制，有效突破了静态性能天花板，展示了智能体系统自我优化和策略自适应迁移的能力。

Abstract: While recent autonomous agents demonstrate impressive capabilities, they predominantly rely on manually scripted workflows and handcrafted heuristics, inherently limiting their potential for open-ended improvement. To address this, we propose Escher-Loop, a fully closed-loop framework that operationalizes the mutual evolution of two distinct populations: Task Agents that solve concrete problems, and Optimizer Agents that recursively refine both the task agents and themselves. To sustain this self-referential evolution, we propose a dynamic benchmarking mechanism that seamlessly reuses the empirical scores of newly generated task agents as relative win-loss signals to update optimizers' scores. This mechanism leverages the evolution of task agents as an inherent signal to drive the evaluation and refinement of optimizers without additional overhead. Empirical evaluations on mathematical optimization problems demonstrate that Escher-Loop effectively pushes past the performance ceilings of static baselines, achieving the highest absolute peak performance across all evaluated tasks under matched compute. Remarkably, we observe that the optimizer agents dynamically adapt their strategies to match the shifting demands of high-performing task agents, which explains the system's continuous improvement and superior late-stage performance.

</details>


### [286] [Do Transaction-Level and Actor-Level AML Queues Agree? An Empirical Evaluation of Granularity Effects on the Elliptic++ Graph](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.23494)
*Ankur Malik*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种评估方法，用于衡量反洗钱系统中交易级与地址级评分粒度对调查队列组成的影响。在固定审查预算下，通过投影框架将交易级分数聚合到地址级，并引入预算化调查指标。基于Elliptic++数据集实验表明：评分粒度显著影响调查队列构成，相同数据与预算下会产生不同的调查地址队列。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 区块链反洗钱系统可在交易和地址两个粒度进行可疑评分，但合规行动实际以地址为单位执行。现有研究缺乏对评分粒度如何影响调查队列构成的量化评估方法，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 构建投影框架，通过四种聚合算子将交易级评分映射到地址级行动单元；引入yield@budget、负担分解和案例碎片化等预算化调查指标；在Elliptic++数据集上使用独立随机森林分类器，分别在因果时序和静态池化协议下训练，通过Jaccard重叠度、负担分解和特征匹配消融实验比较调查队列。

Result: 1%预算下，时序评估Jaccard均值0.374(SD 0.171)；静态评估Jaccard仅0.087(95%CI [0.079,0.094])。富特征地址模型Jaccard=0.051，每100次审查中非法样本占比4.3%，而交易投影队列为30.2%。地址级检测价值在时序上高度集中：两个时间步每100次审查非法占比超91%，静态负担仅3.4%。固定混合策略弱于最优单级队列5.05个百分点。

Conclusion: 评分粒度是AML调查系统中不可忽视的设计变量——相同数据、相同预算下，不同粒度产生不同的调查队列，进而影响实际被调查的地址。建议系统设计者需审慎选择评分粒度并考虑时序动态性。

Abstract: Graph-based anti-money laundering (AML) systems on blockchain networks can score suspicious activity at two granularity levels -- transactions or actor addresses -- yet compliance action is conducted per actor. This paper contributes an evaluation methodology for measuring how scoring granularity affects investigation queue composition under fixed review budgets. We formalize the evaluation through a projection framework mapping transaction-level scores to the actor-level action unit via four aggregation operators, and introduce budgeted investigation metrics -- yield@budget, burden decomposition, and case fragmentation. Using the public Elliptic++ Bitcoin dataset (203,769 transactions; 822,942 address occurrences), we train independent random forest classifiers at each level under a causal temporal protocol and compare review queues through Jaccard overlap, burden decomposition, and feature-matching ablations. At one-percent budget, temporal evaluation yields mean Jaccard of 0.374 (SD 0.171); static pooled evaluation yields 0.087 (95% CI [0.079, 0.094]). An enriched address model receiving all 237 features produces even lower overlap (Jaccard=0.051), with 4.3% illicit per 100 reviews versus 30.2% for the transaction-projected queue. Address-level detection value is temporally concentrated: two timesteps exceed 91% illicit per 100 reviews while the static burden is only 3.4%. A fixed hybrid policy underperforms the best single-level queue by 5.05pp (CI [-10.2pp, -0.9pp]). These findings establish that scoring granularity is a consequential design variable for AML investigation systems -- same data, same budget, different queues, different addresses investigated.

</details>


### [287] [MetaGAI: A Large-Scale and High-Quality Benchmark for Generative AI Model and Data Card Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.23539)
*Haoxuan Zhang,Ruochi Li,Yang Zhang,Zhenni Liang,Junhua Ding,Ting Xiao,Haihua Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MetaGAI是一个包含2,541个验证文档三元组的大规模基准测试集，通过语义三角化构建，用于自动生成模型卡和数据卡的系统评估。采用多智能体框架和四维人类验证，揭示了稀疏MoE架构在成本-质量效率上的优势以及忠实度与完整性之间的根本性权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式AI的快速普及需要严格的文档标准以确保透明度和治理，但手动创建模型卡和数据卡不可扩展，而自动化方法缺乏大规模、高保真的基准测试用于系统评估。

Method: 通过语义三角化（学术论文、GitHub仓库、Hugging Face制品）构建2,541个验证文档三元组；采用多智能体框架（Retriever、Generator、Editor）；四维人类参与验证（含编辑精炼真值的人类评估）；结合自动化指标与LLM-as-a-Judge的评估协议。

Result: 稀疏MoE架构在成本-质量效率上表现最优；存在忠实度与完整性之间的根本性权衡。

Conclusion: MetaGAI为自动化模型卡和数据卡生成方法提供了基础的基准测试、训练和分析平台，数据和代码已开源。

Abstract: The rapid proliferation of Generative AI necessitates rigorous documentation standards for transparency and governance. However, manual creation of Model and Data Cards is not scalable, while automated approaches lack large-scale, high-fidelity benchmarks for systematic evaluation. We introduce MetaGAI, a comprehensive benchmark comprising 2,541 verified document triplets constructed through semantic triangulation of academic papers, GitHub repositories, and Hugging Face artifacts. Unlike prior single-source datasets, MetaGAI employs a multi-agent framework with specialized Retriever, Generator, and Editor agents, validated through four-dimensional human-in-the-loop assessment, including human evaluation of editor-refined ground truth. We establish a robust evaluation protocol combining automated metrics with validated LLM-as-a-Judge frameworks. Extensive analysis reveals that sparse Mixture-of-Experts architectures achieve superior cost-quality efficiency, while a fundamental trade-off exists between faithfulness and completeness. MetaGAI provides a foundational testbed for benchmarking, training, and analyzing automated Model and Data Card generation methods at scale. Our data and code are available at: https://github.com/haoxuan-unt2024/MetaGAI-Benchmark.

</details>


### [288] [FinGround: Detecting and Grounding Financial Hallucinations via Atomic Claim Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.23588)
*Dongxin Guo,Jikun Wu,Siu Ming Yiu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: FinGround是一个三阶段验证-再生成管线，通过金融感知检索、六类声明分解与类型路由验证、以及带引用的重写机制，在检索等化评估下将幻觉率降低68%，全管线相较GPT-4o降低78%；蒸馏后的8B检测器以18倍更低延迟达到91.4% F1，支持$0.003/次查询部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM在金融监管文件问答中频繁编造指标、捏造引用、错误计算衍生量，且现有幻觉检测器对所有声明一视同仁，忽视了43%需要算术重验证的计算错误。随着《欧盟AI法案》高风险执行截止日期（2026年8月）临近，这些问题将带来直接的监管后果。

Method: FinGround采用三阶段管线：Stage 1进行金融感知的混合检索（文本+表格）；Stage 2将答案分解为原子声明，按六类金融分类法标注，并使用类型路由策略（含公式重构）进行验证；Stage 3用段落级和表格单元格级引用重写未支持的声明。同时提出检索等化评估标准方法以隔离验证与检索的效果。

Result: 在检索等化条件下，FinGround将幻觉率相较于最强基线降低68%（p<0.01）；完整管线相较于GPT-4o降低78%。蒸馏的8B检测器以18倍更低延迟达到91.4% F1，部署成本为$0.003/次查询。四周分析师试点提供定性信号支持。

Conclusion: FinGround通过类型感知的声明验证与检索等化评估，显著降低了金融文档问答中的幻觉率，验证了结构化验证对计算错误类型的必要性，并展示了高效的蒸馏部署可行性。

Abstract: Financial AI systems must produce answers grounded in specific regulatory filings, yet current LLMs fabricate metrics, invent citations, and miscalculate derived quantities. These errors carry direct regulatory consequences as the EU AI Act's high-risk enforcement deadline approaches (August 2026). Existing hallucination detectors treat all claims uniformly, missing 43% of computational errors that require arithmetic re-verification against structured tables. We present FinGround, a three-stage verify-then-ground pipeline for financial document QA. Stage 1 performs finance-aware hybrid retrieval over text and tables. Stage 2 decomposes answers into atomic claims classified by a six-type financial taxonomy and verified with type-routed strategies including formula reconstruction. Stage 3 rewrites unsupported claims with paragraph- and table-cell-level citations. To cleanly isolate verification value from retrieval quality, we propose retrieval-equalized evaluation as standard methodology for RAG verification research: when all systems receive identical retrieval, FinGround still reduces hallucination rates by 68% over the strongest baseline ($p < 0.01$). The full pipeline achieves a 78% reduction relative to GPT-4o. An 8B distilled detector retains 91.4% F1 at 18x lower per-claim latency, enabling $0.003/query deployment, supported by qualitative signals from a four-week analyst pilot.

</details>


### [289] [When AI reviews science: Can we trust the referee?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.23593)
*Jialiang Wang,Yuchen Liu,Hang Xu,Kaichun Hu,Shimin Di,Wangze Ni,Linan Yue,Min-Ling Zhang,Kui Ren,Lei Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文对AI审稿的安全性进行了系统分析，构建了覆盖审稿全生命周期的攻击分类体系，并通过控制实验揭示了声望框架、断言强度、回复谄媚和上下文投毒等攻击对LLM审稿评分的因果影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学投稿量持续增长，超出人工审稿能力；LLM被引入审稿流程但暴露出严重的安全漏洞（如提示注入、对抗性措辞、权威偏见、幻觉等），亟需系统性评估AI审稿的可靠性。

Method: 提出覆盖训练/数据检索、桌面审稿、深度审稿、回复反驳、系统级等阶段的攻击分类体系；在ICLR 2025分层样本上使用两种先进LLM审稿人进行四项处理-控制探针实验，分别测试声望框架、断言强度、回复谄媚和上下文投毒的因果效应。

Result: 声望框架、断言强度、回复谄媚和上下文投毒均对LLM审稿评分产生显著因果影响，揭示了AI审稿系统在多个维度上的脆弱性。

Conclusion: 该分类体系和实验审计为评估和追踪AI审稿可靠性提供了循证基线，并指出了具体失效点以指导可测试的针对性缓解措施。
